## Narration 451

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1388)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 33</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ لِيَهُودِ خَيْبَرَ يَوْمَ افْتَتَحَ خَيْبَرَ ‏ &quot;‏ أُقِرُّكُمْ فِيهَا مَا أَقَرَّكُمُ اللَّهُ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ عَلَى أَنَّ الثَّمَرَ بَيْنَنَا وَبَيْنَكُمْ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ فَكَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَبْعَثُ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ رَوَاحَةَ فَيَخْرُصُ بَيْنَهُ وَبَيْنَهُمْ ثُمَّ يَقُولُ إِنْ شِئْتُمْ فَلَكُمْ وَإِنْ شِئْتُمْ فَلِيَ ‏.‏ فَكَانُوا يَأْخُذُونَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1388]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1388" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said, &quot;The recognised and permitted form of qirad is that a man take capital from an associate to use. He does not guarantee it and in travelling pays out of the capital for food and clothes and what he makes good use of, according to the amount of capital. That is, when he travels to do the work and the capital can support it. If he remains with his people, he does not have expenses or clothing from the capital.&quot; Malik said, &quot;There is no harm in the two parties in a qirad helping each other by way of a favour when it is acceptable to them both.&quot; Malik said, &quot;There is no harm in the investor of the capital buying some of the goods from the agent in the qirad if that is acceptable and without conditions.&quot; Malik spoke about an investor making a qirad loan to a man and his slave, to be used by both. He said, &quot;That is permitted, and there is no harm in it because the profit is property for his slave, and the profit is not for the master until he takes it from him. It is like the rest of his earnings.&quot; Malik said, &quot;When a man owes money to another man and he asks him to let it stay with him as a quirad, that is disapproved of until the creditor receives his property. Then he can make it a qirad loan or keep it. That is because the debtor may be in a tight situation, and want to delay it to increase it for him.&quot; Malik spoke about an investor who made a qirad loan to a man, and some of the principal was lost before he used it, and then he used it and made a profit. The agent wanted to make the principal the remainder of the money after what was lost from it. Malik said, &quot;His statement is not accepted, and the principal is made up to its original amount from his profit. Then they divide what remains after the principal has been repaid according to the conditions of the qirad.&quot; Malik said, &quot;Qirad loan is only good in gold or silver coin and it is never permitted in any kind of wares or goods or articles.&quot; Malik said, &quot;There are certain transactions which if a long span of time passes after the transaction takes place, its revocation becomes unacceptable. As for usury, there is never anything except its rejection whether it is a little or a lot. What is permitted in other than it is not permitted in it because Allah, the Blessed and the Exalted, said in His Book, &#x27;If you repent, you have your capital back, not wronging and not wronged. &#x27; &quot; 32.4 Conditions Permitted in Qirad Yahya said that Malik spoke about an investor who made a qirad loan and stipulated to the agent that only certain goods should be bought with his money or he forbade certain goods which he named to be bought. He said, &quot;There is no harm in an investor making a condition on an agent in qirad not to buy a certain kind of animal or goods which he specifies. It is disapproved of for an investor to make as a condition on an agent in qirad that he only buy certain goods unless the goods which he orders him to buy are in plentiful supply and do not fail either in winter or summer. There is no harm in that case.&quot; Malik spoke about an investor who loaned qirad money and stipulated that something of the profit should be his alone without the agent sharing in it. He said, &quot;That is not good, even if it is only one dirham unless he stipulates that half the profit is his and half the profit is the agent&#x27;s or a third or a fourth or whatever. When he names a percentage, whether great or small, everything specified by that is halal. This is the qirad of the muslims.&quot; He said, &quot;It is also not good if the investor stipulates that one dirham or more of the profit is purely his, with out the agent sharing it and then what remains of the profit is to be divided in half between them. That is not the qirad of the Muslims.&quot; Yahya said that Malik said, &quot;The person who puts up the principal must not stipulate that he has something of the profit alone without the agent sharing in it, nor must the agent stipulate that he has something of the profit alone without the investor sharing. In qirad, there is no sale, no rent, no work, no advance, and no convenience which one party specifies to himself without the other party sharing unless one party allows it to the other unconditionally as a favour and that is alright to both. Neither of the parties should make a condition over the other which increases him in gold or silver or food over the other party.&quot; He said, &quot;If any of that enters the qirad, it becomes hire, and hire is only good with known and fixed terms. The agent should not stipulate when he takes the principal that he repay or commission anyone with the goods, nor that he take any of them for himself. When there is a profit, and it is time to separate the capital, then they divide the profit according to the terms of the contract. If the principal does not increase or there is a loss, the agent does not have to make up for what he spent on himself or for the loss. That falls to the investor from the principal. Qirad is permitted upon whatever terms the investor and the agent make a mutual agreement, of half the profit, or a third or a fourth or whatever.&quot; Malik said, &quot;It is not permitted for the agent to stipulate that he use the qirad money for a certain number of years and that it not be taken from him during that time.&quot; He said, &quot;It is not good for the investor to stipulate that the qirad money should not be returned for a certain number of years which are specified, because the qirad is not for a term. The investor loans it to an agent to use for him. If it seems proper to either of them to abandon the project and the money is coin, and nothing has been bought with it, it can be abandoned, and the investor takes his money back. If it seems proper to the investor to take the qirad loan back after goods have been purchased with it, he cannot do so until the buyer has sold the goods and they have become money. If it seems proper to the agent to return the loan, and it has been turned to goods he cannot do so until he has sold them. He returns the loan in cash as he took it.&quot; Malik said, &quot;It is not good for the investor to stipulate that the agent pay any zakat due from his portion of the profit in particular, because the investor by stipulating that, stipulates fixed increase for himself from the profit because the portion of zakat he would be liable for by his portion of the profit, is removed from him. &quot;It is not permitted for the investor to stipulate to the agent to only buy from so-and-so, referring to a specific man. That is not permitted because by doing so he would become his hireling for a wage.&quot; Malik spoke about an investor in qirad who stipulated a guarantee for an amount of money from the agent, &quot;The investor is not permitted to stipulate conditions about his principal other than the conditions on which qirad is based or according to the precedent of the sunna of the Muslims. If the principal is increased by the condition of guarantee, the investor has increased his share of the profit because of the position of the guarantee. But the profit is only to be divided according to what it would have been had the loan been given without the guarantee. If the principal is destroyed, I do not think that the agent has a guarantee held against him because the stipulation of guarantees in qirad is null and void.&quot; Malik spoke about an investor who gave qirad money to a man and the man stipulated that he would only buy palms or animals with it because he sought to eat the dates or the offspring of the animals and he kept them for some time to use for himself. He said, &quot;That is not permitted. It is not the sunna of the Muslims in qirad unless he buys it and then sells it as other goods are sold.&quot; Malik said, &quot;There is no harm in the agent stipulating on the investor a slave to help him provided that the slave stands to gain along with him out of the investment, and when the slave only helps him with the investment, not with anything else.&quot; Yahya said that Malik said, &quot;No one should make a qirad loan except in coin, because the loan must not be in wares, since loaning wares can only be worked in one of two ways:Either the owner of the wares says to the borrower, &#x27;Take these wares and sell them. Buy and sell with the capital realized according to qirad.&#x27; The investor stipulates increase for himself from the sale of his goods and what relieves him of expenses in selling it. Or else he says, &#x27;Barter with these goods and sell. When you are through, buy for me the like of my goods which I gave you. If there is increase, it is between you and me. &#x27;It may happen that the investor gives the goods to the agent at a time in which they are in demand and expensive, and then the agent returns them while they are cheap and he might have bought them for only a third of the original price or even less than that. The agent then has a profit of half the amount by which the price of the wares has decreased as his portion of the profit. Or he might take the wares at a time when their price is low, and make use of them until he has a lot of money. Then those wares become expensive and their price rises when he returns them, so he buys them for all that he has so that all his work and concern have been in vain. This is an uncertain transaction and is not good. If, however, that is not known until it has happened, then the wage an agent in qirad would be paid for selling that, is looked at and he is given it for his concern. Then the money is qirad from the day the money became cash and collected as coin and it is returned as a qirad like that.&quot; Yahya said that Malik spoke about a man who made a qirad loan to a man and he bought wares with it and transported them to a commercial centre. It was not profitable to sell them and the agent feared a loss if he sold them, so he hired transport to take them to another city, and he sold them there and made a loss, and the cost of the hire was greater than the principal. Malik said, &quot;If the agent can pay the cost of the hire from what the capital realized, his way is that. Whatever portion of the hire is not covered by the principal, the agent must pay it. The investor is not answerable for any of it. That is because the investor only ordered him to trade with the principal. The investor is not answerable for other than the principal. Had the investor been liable, it would have been an additional loss to him on top of the principal which he invested. The agent cannot put that on to the investor.&quot; Yahya said that Malik spoke about an investor who made a qirad loan to a man, who used it and made a profit. Then the man bought with all the profit a slave-girl and he had intercourse with her and she became pregnant by him, and so the capital decreased. Malik said, &quot;If he has money, the price of the slave-girl is taken from his property, and the capital is restored by it. If there is something left over after the money is paid, it is divided between them according to the first qirad. If he cannot pay it, the slave-girl is sold so that the capital is restored from her price.&quot; Malik spoke about an investor who made a qirad loan to a man, and the agent spent more than the amount of the qirad loan when buying goods with it and paid the increase from his own money. Malik said, &quot;The investor has a choice if the goods are sold for a profit or loss or if they are not sold. If he wishes to take the goods, he takes them and pays the agent back what he put in for them. If the agent refuses, the investor is a partner for his share of the price in increase and decrease according to what the agent paid extra for them from himself.&quot; Malik spoke about an agent who took qirad money from a man and then gave it to another man to use as a qirad without the consent of the investor. He said, &quot;The agent is responsible for the property. If it is decreased, he is responsible for the loss. If there is profit, the investor has his stipulation of the profit, and then the agent has his stipulation of what remains of the money.&quot; Malik spoke about an agent who exceeded and borrowed some of what he had of qirad in money and he bought goods for himself with it. Malik said, &quot;If he has a profit, the profit is divided according to the condition between them in the qirad. If he has a loss, he is responsible for the loss.&quot; Malik said about an investor who paid qirad money to a man, and the agent borrowed some of the cash and bought goods for himself with it, &quot;The investor of the capital has a choice. If he wishes, he shares with him in the goods according to the qirad, and if he wishes, he frees himself of them, and takes all of the principal back from the agent. That is what is done with some one who oversteps.&quot; Yahya said that Malik spoke about an investor who made a qirad loan to a man. He said, &quot;When the investment is large, the travelling expenses of the agent are taken from it. He can use it to eat and clothe himself in an acceptable fashion according to the size of the investment. If it saves him trouble, he can take a wage from some of the capital, if it is large, and he cannot support himself. There are certain jobs which an agent or his like are not responsible for, amongst them are collecting debts, transporting the goods, loading up and so forth. He can hire from the capital someone to do that for him. The agent should not spend from the capital nor clothe himself from it while he resides with his family. It is only permitted for him to have expenses when he travels for the investment. The expenses are taken from the capital. If he is only trading with the property in the city in which he resides, he has no expenses from the capital and no clothing.&quot; Malik spoke about an investor who paid qirad money to a man, and the agent went out with it and with his own capital. He said, &quot;The expenses come from the qirad and from his own capital according to their proportions.&quot; Yahya said that Malik spoke about an agent who had qirad money with him and he spent from it and clothed himself. He said, &quot;He cannot give away any of it, and neither a beggar nor anyone else is to be given any of it and he does not pay anyone compensation from it. If he meets some people, and they bring out food and he brings out food, I hope that that will be permitted to him if he does not intend to bestow something on them. If he intends that or what is like that without the permission of the investor, he must get the sanction of the investor for it. If he sanctions it, there is no harm. If he refuses to sanction it, he must repay it with like if he has something which is suitable as compensation.&quot; Yahya said that Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed on way of doing things among us about an investor who pays qirad money to an agent to buy goods, and the agent then sells the goods for a price to be paid later, and has a profit in the transaction, then the agent dies before he has received payment, is that if his heirs want to take that money, they have their father&#x27;s stipulated portion from the profit. That is theirs if they are trustworthy to take the payment. If they dislike to collect it from the debtor and they refer him to the investor, they are not obliged to collect it and there is nothing against them and nothing for them by their surrendering it to the investor. If they do collect it, they have a share of it and expenses like their father had. They are in the position of their father. If they are not trustworthy to do so, they can bring someone reliable and trustworthy to collect the money. If he collects all the capital and all the profit, they are in the position of their father.&quot; Malik spoke about an investor who paid qirad money to a man provided that he used it and was responsible for any delayed payment for which he sold it. He said, &quot;This is obligatory on the agent. If he sells it for delayed payment, he is responsible for it.&quot; Yahya said that Malik spoke about an investor who gave qirad money to a man, and then the man sought a loan from the investor or the investor borrowed money from the agent, or the investor left goods with the agent to sell for him, or the investor gave the agent dinars to buy goods with. Malik said, &quot;There is no harm if the investor leaves his goods with him knowing that if the agent did not have his money and he had asked a similar thing of him, he would have still done it because of the brotherhood between them or because it would have been no bother to him and that had the agent refused that, he would not have removed his capital from him. Or if the agent had borrowed from the investor or carried his goods for him and he knew that if the investor had not had his capital with him, he would have still done the same for him, and had he refused that to him, he would not have returned his capital to him. If that is true between both of them and it is in the way of a favour between them and it is not a condition in the terms of the qirad, it is permitted and there is no harm in it. If a condition comes into it, or it is feared that the agent is only doing it for the investor in order to safeguard the capital in his possession, or the investor is only doing it because the agent has taken his capital and will not return it to him, that is not permitted in qirad and it is part of what the people of knowledge forbid.&#x27; &quot; Yahya said that Malik spoke about a man who loaned another man money and then the debtor asked him to leave it with him as a qirad. Malik said, &quot;I do not like that unless he takes his money back from him, and then pays it to him as a qirad if he wishes or if he wishes keep it.&quot; Malik spoke about an investor who paid a man qirad money and the man told him that it was collected with him and asked him to write it for him as a loan. He said, &quot;I do not like that unless he takes his money from him and then lends it to him or keeps it as he wishes. That is only out of fear that he has lost some of it, and wants to defer it so that he can make up what has been lost of it. That is disapproved of and is not permitted and it is not good.&quot; Yahya said that Malik spoke about an investor paying qirad money to an agent who made a profit and then wanted to take his share of the profit and the investor was away. He said, &quot;He should not take any of it unless the investor is present. If he takes something from it, he is responsible for it until it is accounted for in the division of the capital.&quot; Malik said, &quot;It is not permitted for the parties involved in a qirad to account and divide property which is away from them until the capital is present, and the investor is given the principal in full. Then they divide the profit into their agreed portions.&quot; Malik spoke about a man taking qirad money, and buying goods with it while he had a debt. His creditors sought and found him while he was in a city away from the investor, and he had profitable merchandise whose good quality was clear. They wanted him to sell the merchandise for them so that they could take his share of the profit. Malik said, &quot;None of the profit of the qirad is taken until the investor is present. He takes his principal and then the profit is divided mutually between them.&quot; Malik spoke about an investor who put qirad money with an agent and he used it and had a profit. Then the principal was set aside and the profit divided. He took his share and added the share of the investor to his principal in the presence of witnesses he had called. Malik said, &quot;It is not permitted to divide the profit unless the investor is present. If he has taken something here turns it until the investor has received the principal in full. Then what remains is divided into their respective portions.&quot; Malik spoke about an investor who put qirad money with an agent. The agent used it and then came to the investor and said, &quot;This is your portion of the profit, and I have taken the like of it for myself, and I have retained your principal in full.&quot; Malik said, &quot;I do not like that, unless all the capital is present, the principal is there and he knows that it is complete and he receives it. Then they divide the profit between them. He returns the principal to him if he wishes, or he keeps it. The presence of the principal is necessary out of fear that the agent might have lost some of it, and so may want it not to be removed from him and to keep it in his hand.&quot; Yahya said that Malik spoke about an investor who put qirad money with an agent who bought goods with it, and the investor told him to sell them. The agent said that he did not see any way to sell at that time and they quarrelled about it. He said, &quot;One does not look at the statement of either of them. The people of experience and insight concerning such goods are asked about these goods. If they can see anyway of selling them they are sold for them. If they think it is time to wait, they should wait.&quot; Malik spoke about a man who took qirad money from an investor and used it and when the investor asked him for his money, he said that he had it in full. When he held him to his settlement he admitted that &quot;Such-and-such of it was lost with me,&quot; and he named an amount of money. &quot;I told you that so that you would leave it with me.&quot; Malik said, &quot;He does not benefit by denying it after he had confirmed that he had it all . He is answerable by his confession against himself unless he produces evidence about the loss of that property which confirms his statement. If he does not produce an acceptable reason he is answerable by his confession, and his denial does not avail him.&quot; Malik said, &quot;Similarly, had he said, &#x27;I have had such-and-such a profit from the capital,&#x27; and then the owner of the capital asked him to pay him the principal and his profit, and he said that he had not had any profit in it and had said that only so it might be left in his possession, it does not benefit him. He is taken to account for what he affirmed unless he brings acceptable proof of his word, so that the first statement is not binding on him.&quot; Malik spoke about an investor who put qirad money with an agent who made a profit with it. The agent said, &quot;I took the qirad from you provided that I would have two-thirds.&quot; The owner of the capital says, &quot;I gave you a qirad provided that you had a third.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The word is the word of the agent, and he must take an oath on that if what he says resembles the known practice of qirad or is close to it. If he brings a matter which is unacceptable and people do not make qirads like that, he is not believed, and it is judged to be according to how a qirad like it would normally be.&quot; Malik spoke about a man who gave a man one hundred dinars as a qirad. He bought goods with it and then went to pay the one hundred dinars to the owner of the goods and found that they had been stolen. The investor says, &quot;Sell the goods. If there is anything over, it is mine. If there is a loss, it is against you because you lost it.&quot; The agent says, &quot;Rather you must fulfil what the seller is owed. I bought them with your capital which you gave me.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The agent is obliged to pay the price to the seller and the investor is told, &#x27;If you wish, pay the hundred dinars to the agent and the goods are between you. The qirad is according to what the first hundred was based on. If you wish, you are free of the goods.&#x27; If the hundred dinars are paid to the agent, it is a qirad according to the conditions of the first qirad. If he refuses, the goods belong to the agent and he must pay their price.&quot; Malik spoke about two people in a qirad who settled up and the agent still had some of the goods which he used - threadbare cloth or a waterskin or the like of that. Malik said, &quot;Any of that which is insignificant is of no importance and belongs to the agent. I have not heard anyone give a decision calling for the return of that. Anything which has a price is returned. If it is something which has value like an animal, camel, coarse cloth or the like of that which fetches a price, I think that he should return what he has remaining of such things unless the owner overlooks it.&quot; Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Said ibn al- Musayyab that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said to the jews of Khaybar on the day of the conquest of Khaybar, &quot;I confirm you in it as long as Allah, the Mighty, the Majestic, establishes you in it, provided that the fruits are divided between us and you.&quot; Said continued, &quot;The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, used to send Abdullah ibn Rawaha, to assess the division of the fruit crop between him and them, and he would say, &#x27;If you wish, you can buy it back, and if you wish, it is mine.&#x27; They would take it</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">सईद बिन मुसय्यब से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने खैबर के यहूदियों से फ़रमाया: जब तक अल्लाह चाहेगा मैं तुम्हें यहाँ रहने दूँगा, इस शर्त पर के जो फल पैदा होंगे वो हम में और तुम में शरीक होंगे। रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) अब्दुल्लाह बिन रवाहा (रदी अल्लाहू अन्हु) को भेजते जो फल का अंदाज़ा (खिरास) करते, फिर यहूदी चाहो तो फल ले लेते और आधा हिस्सा मुसलमानों को दे देते।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1388]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">سعید بن مسیب سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا خبیر کے یہودیوں سے جس دن خبیر فتح ہوا جو تم کو اللہ نے دیا ہے اس پر میں تہیں برقرار رکھوں گا اس شرط سے کہ جتنے پھل یہاں پیدا ہوتے ہیں وہ ہم میں تم میں مشترک ہوں تو رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم عبداللہ بن رواضہ کو بھیجتے تھے وہ درختوں کو دیکھ کر ان کے پھلوں کا اندازہ کرتے تھے اگر تم چاہو تو تم ان پھولوں کو لے لو اور جو اندازہ ہوا ہے اس کا آدھا ہم کو دیدو ہم تم کو اس اندازے کے آدھے پھل دیں گے یہود خود پھل لے لیا کرتے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1388]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1388" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Saeed bin Musayyib se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne Khaibar ke yahudiyon se farmaya: Jab tak Allah chahega main tumhein yahan rehne doonga, is shart par ke jo phal paida honge wo hum mein aur tum mein shareek honge. Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) Abdullah bin Rawaha (Radi Allahu Anhu) ko bhejte jo phal ka andaza (khiraas) karte, phir yahudi chaho to phal le lete aur aadha hissa musalmanon ko de dete.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1388]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1388" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 452

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1390)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 34</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ رَبِيعَةَ بْنِ أَبِي عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ حَنْظَلَةَ بْنِ قَيْسٍ الزُّرَقِيِّ، عَنْ رَافِعِ بْنِ خَدِيجٍ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم نَهَى عَنْ كِرَاءِ الْمَزَارِعِ قَالَ حَنْظَلَةُ فَسَأَلْتُ رَافِعَ بْنَ خَدِيجٍ بِالذَّهَبِ وَالْوَرِقِ فَقَالَ أَمَّا بِالذَّهَبِ وَالْوَرِقِ فَلاَ بَأْسَ بِهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1390]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1390" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">रफ़ी बिन ख़दीज (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने खेत किराए पर देने (मुहकला) से मना फ़रमाया. हन्ज़ला ने पूछा: अगर सोने या चांदी के बदले किराया लिया जाये? कहा इस में कोई हर्ज नहीं.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1390]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1390" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">رافع بن خدیج سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے منع کیا کھیتوں کے کرایہ دینے سے حنظلہ نے کہا میں نے رافع سے پوچھا اگر سونے یا چاندی کے بدلے میں کرایہ کر دے انہوں نے کہا کچھ قباحت نہیں ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1390]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1390" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Rafi bin Khadeej (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne khet kiraye par dene (muhaqala) se mana farmaya. Hanzala ne poochha: Agar sone ya chandi ke badle kiraya liya jaye? Kaha is mein koi haraj nahi.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1390]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1390" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 453

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1395)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 35</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، وَعَنْ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ عَوْفٍ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَضَى بِالشُّفْعَةِ فِيمَا لَمْ يُقْسَمْ بَيْنَ الشُّرَكَاءِ فَإِذَا وَقَعَتِ الْحُدُودُ بَيْنَهُمْ فَلاَ شُفْعَةَ فِيهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1395]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1395" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1395</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1395 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Said ibn al- Musayyab and from Abu Salama ibn Abd ar-Rahman ibn Awf that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, decreed for partners the right of preemption in property which had not been divided up. When boundaries had been fixed between them, then there was no right of pre-emption</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1395]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1395" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1395</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1395 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">य़ह्या ने मुझसे रिवायत की, इमाम मालिक से, इब्न शिहाब से, सईद इब्न अल-मुसय्यब और अबू सलमा इब्न अब्द अर-रहमान इब्न औफ़ से के रसूल अल्लाह सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम ने हिस्सा दारन के लिए उस जायदाद में हक़-ए-शुफ़ा का फ़ैसला फ़रमाया जो अभी तक़सीम नहीं हुई थी। जब उनके दरमियान हद-बंदी कर दी जाए, तो फिर कोई हक़-ए-शुफ़ा नहीं है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1395]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1395" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھ سے روایت کی، امام مالک سے، ابن شہاب سے، سعید بن مسیب اور ابو سلمہ بن عبدالرحمن بن عوف سے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے حصہ داروں کے لیے اس جائیداد میں حق شفعہ کا فیصلہ فرمایا جو ابھی تقسیم نہیں ہوئی تھی۔ جب ان کے درمیان حد بندی کر دی جائے، تو پھر کوئی حق شفعہ نہیں ہے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1395]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1395" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujhse riwayat ki, Imam Malik se, Ibn Shihab se, Said ibn al-Musayyab aur Abu Salama ibn Abd ar-Rahman ibn Awf se ke Rasool Allah Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam ne hissa daron ke liye us jaidad mein haq-e-shufa ka faisla farmaya jo abhi taqseem nahi hui thi. Jab unke darmiyan had-bandi kar di jaye, to phir koi haq-e-shufa nahi hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1395]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1395" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 454

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1399)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ زَيْنَبَ بِنْتِ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ، عَنْ أُمِّ سَلَمَةَ، زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ إِنَّمَا أَنَا بَشَرٌ وَإِنَّكُمْ تَخْتَصِمُونَ إِلَىَّ فَلَعَلَّ بَعْضَكُمْ أَنْ يَكُونَ أَلْحَنَ بِحُجَّتِهِ مِنْ بَعْضٍ فَأَقْضِيَ لَهُ عَلَى نَحْوِ مَا أَسْمَعُ مِنْهُ فَمَنْ قَضَيْتُ لَهُ بِشَىْءٍ مِنْ حَقِّ أَخِيهِ فَلاَ يَأْخُذَنَّ مِنْهُ شَيْئًا فَإِنَّمَا أَقْطَعُ لَهُ قِطْعَةً مِنَ النَّارِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1399]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1399" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1399 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Hisham ibn Urwa from his father from Zaynab bint Abi Salama from Umm Salama, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;I am but a man to whom you bring your disputes. Perhaps one of you is more eloquent in his proof than the other, so I give judgement according to what I have heard from him. Whatever I decide for him which is part of the right of his brother, he must not take any of it, for I am granting him a portion of the Fire</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1399]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1399" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">उम्म-ए-सलमा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हा) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: मैं भी बशर हूं और तुम मेरे पास लड़ते झगड़ते आते हो, शायद तुम में से कोई बातें बना कर अपने दा&#x27;वे को साबित कर ले, फिर मैं उसके मुताबिक़ फ़ैसला कर दूं। तो जिस शख्श को मैं उसके भाई का हक़ दिला दूं, वो उसे न ले क्योंकि मैं उसे आग का एक अंगारा दिला रहा हूं।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1399]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1399" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ام سلمہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہا سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا میں بھی بشر ہوں اور تم میرے پاس لڑتے جھگڑتے آتے ہو شائد تم میں سے کوئی باتیں بنا کر اپنے دعوے کو ثابت کرلے پھر میں اس کے موافق فیصلہ کروں اس کے کہنے پر تو جس شخص کو میں اس کے بھائی حقل دلا دوں وہ نہ لے کیونکہ میں ایک انگارہ آگ کا اس کو دلاتا ہوں ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1399]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1399" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Umm-e-Salama (Radi Allahu Anha) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Main bhi bashar hoon aur tum mere paas ladte jhagadte aate ho, shayad tum mein se koi baatein bana kar apne da&#x27;we ko sabit kar le, phir main uske mutabiq faisla kar doon. Toh jis shakhs ko main uske bhai ka haq dila doon, wo usey na le kyunki main usey aag ka ek angara dila raha hoon.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1399]</p>

## Narration 455

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1401)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ حَزْمٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ عُثْمَانَ، عَنْ أَبِي عَمْرَةَ الأَنْصَارِيِّ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ خَالِدٍ الْجُهَنِيِّ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ أَلاَ أُخْبِرُكُمْ بِخَيْرِ الشُّهَدَاءِ الَّذِي يَأْتِي بِشَهَادَتِهِ قَبْلَ أَنْ يُسْأَلَهَا أَوْ يُخْبِرُ بِشَهَادَتِهِ قَبْلَ أَنْ يُسْأَلَهَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1401]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1401" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1401</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1401 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abdullah ibn Abi Bakr ibn Muhammad ibn Amr ibn Hazm from his father from Abdullah ibn Amr ibn Uthman from Abu Amra al-Ansari from Zayd ibn Khalid al-Juhani that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Shall I not tell you who is the best of witnesses? The one who brings his testimony before he is asked for it, or tells his testimony before he is asked for it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1401]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1401" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1401</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1401 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">ज़ैद बिन खालिद अल-जुहनी (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: क्या मैं तुम्हें सब से बेहतर गवाह की खबर न दूं? जो गवाही देता है इस से पहले के उस से पूछा जाये.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1401]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1401" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">زید بن خالد جہنی سے روایت ہے کہ فرمایا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے کیا نہ خبر دوں میں تم کو سب سے بہتر گواہ کی جو گواہی دیتا ہے قبل اس کے کہ پوچھا جائے اس سے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1401]</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Zaid bin Khalid Al-Juhani (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Kya main tumhein sab se behtar gawah ki khabar na doon? Jo gawahi deta hai is se pehle ke us se poocha jaye.</p>

## Narration 456

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1406)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">قَالَ يَحْيَى قَالَ مَالِكٌ عَنْ جَعْفَرِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَضَى بِالْيَمِينِ مَعَ الشَّاهِدِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1406]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1406" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said, &quot;Malik said from Jafar ibn Muhammad from his father that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, pronounced judgement on the basis of an oath with one witness</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1406]</p>

## Narration 457

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1411)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">قَالَ يَحْيَى حَدَّثَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ هَاشِمِ بْنِ هَاشِمِ بْنِ عُتْبَةَ بْنِ أَبِي وَقَّاصٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ نِسْطَاسٍ، عَنْ جَابِرِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ الأَنْصَارِيِّ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مَنْ حَلَفَ عَلَى مِنْبَرِي آثِمًا تَبَوَّأَ مَقْعَدَهُ مِنَ النَّارِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1411]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1411" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1411</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1411 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said, Malik related to us from Hisham ibn Hisham ibn Utba ibn Abi Waqqas from Abdullah ibn Nistas from Jabir ibn Abdullah al- Ansari that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;If someone swears a false oath near this mimbar of mine, he will take his seat in the fire</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1411]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1411" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">जाबिर बिन अब्दुल्लाह (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया जो शख्स मेरे मिंबर पर झूठी कसम खाए, उसने अपना ठिकाना बना लिया जहन्नुम में।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1411]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1411" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">جابر بن عبداللہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا جو شخص میرے منبر پر جھوٹی قسم کھائے اس نے اپنا ٹھکانہ نبالیا جہنم میں</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1411]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1411" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1411]</p>

## Narration 458

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1412)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنِ الْعَلاَءِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ مَعْبَدِ بْنِ كَعْبٍ السَّلَمِيِّ، عَنْ أَخِيهِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ كَعْبِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ الأَنْصَارِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِي أُمَامَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ مَنِ اقْتَطَعَ حَقَّ امْرِئٍ مُسْلِمٍ بِيَمِينِهِ حَرَّمَ اللَّهُ عَلَيْهِ الْجَنَّةَ وَأَوْجَبَ لَهُ النَّارَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالُوا وَإِنْ كَانَ شَيْئًا يَسِيرًا يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ وَإِنْ كَانَ قَضِيبًا مِنْ أَرَاكٍ وَإِنْ كَانَ قَضِيبًا مِنْ أَرَاكٍ وَإِنْ كَانَ قَضِيبًا مِنْ أَرَاكٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَهَا ثَلاَثَ مَرَّاتٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1412]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1412" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1412</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1412 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from al-Ala ibn Abd ar-Rahman from Mabad ibn Kab as-Salami from his brother Abdullah ibn Kab ibn Malik al-Ansari from Abu Umama that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Whoever cuts off the right of a muslim man by his oath, Allah forbids him the Garden and obliges the Fire for him.&quot; They said, &quot;Even if it is something insignificant, Messenger of Allah?&quot; He said, &quot;Even if it is a tooth-stick, even if it is a tooth- stick,&quot; repeating it three times</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1412]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1412" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1412</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1412 ]</p>

</details>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मालिक ने मुझे अल-अला इब्न अब्द अर-रहमान से, उन्होंने मबाद इब्न काब अस-सलमी से, उन्होंने अपने भाई अब्दुल्लाह इब्न काब इब्न मालिक अल-अंसारी से, उन्होंने अबू उमामा से रिवायत की के अल्लाह के रसूल (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया: जो शख्स अपनी कसम के ज़रिए किसी मुसलमान का हक मार ले, अल्लाह उस पर जन्नत हराम कर देता है और जहन्नुम वाजिब कर देता है। उन्होंने अर्ज़ किया: ऐ अल्लाह के रसूल! अगरचे वो कोई मामूली चीज़ ही क्यों न हो? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया: अगरचे वो मिसवाक ही क्यों न हो, अगरचे वो मिसवाक ही क्यों न हो (आप ने तीन बार फरमाया)।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1412]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1412" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">امام مالک نے مجھے العلاء بن عبدالرحمن سے، انہوں نے معبد بن کعب سلمیٰ سے، انہوں نے اپنے بھائی عبداللہ بن کعب بن مالک انصاری سے، انہوں نے ابو امامہ سے روایت کی کہ اللہ کے رسول صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: جو شخص اپنی قسم کے ذریعے کسی مسلمان کا حق مار لے، اللہ اس پر جنت حرام کر دیتا ہے اور جہنم واجب کر دیتا ہے۔ انہوں نے عرض کیا: اے اللہ کے رسول! اگرچہ وہ کوئی معمولی چیز ہی کیوں نہ ہو؟ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: اگرچہ وہ مسواک ہی کیوں نہ ہو، اگرچہ وہ مسواک ہی کیوں نہ ہو (آپ نے تین بار فرمایا)۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1412]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1412" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Imam Malik ne mujhe al-Ala ibn Abd ar-Rahman se, unhon ne Mabad ibn Kab as-Salami se, unhon ne apne bhai Abdullah ibn Kab ibn Malik al-Ansari se, unhon ne Abu Umama se riwayat ki ke Allah ke Rasool (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Jo shakhs apni qasam ke zariye kisi musalman ka haq maar le, Allah us par jannat haram kar deta hai aur jahannum wajib kar deta hai. Unhon ne arz kiya: Aye Allah ke Rasool! Agarcha wo koi mamooli cheez hi kyun na ho? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Agarcha wo miswak hi kyun na ho, agarcha wo miswak hi kyun na ho (aap ne teen bar farmaya).</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1412]</p>

## Narration 459

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1414)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">قَالَ يَحْيَى حَدَّثَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ يَغْلَقُ الرَّهْنُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1414]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1414" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1414</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1414 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said, &quot;Malik related to us from Ibn Shihab from Sa&#x27;id ibn al-Musayyab that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;The pledge given as security is not forfeited.&#x27; &quot; Malik said, &quot;The explanation of that according to what we think - and Allah knows best - is that a man gives a pledge to somebody in security for something. The pledge is superior to that for which he pawned it. The pledger says to the pawn-broker, &#x27;I will bring you your due, after such-and-such a time. If not, the pledge is yours for what it was pawned for.&#x27; &quot; Malik said, &quot;This transaction is not good and it is not halal. This is what was forbidden. If the owner brings what he pledged it for after the period, it is his. I think that the time condition is void</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1414]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1414" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1414</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1414 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">यहया ने कहा के इमाम मालिक ने हमें इब्न शिहाब से, उन्होंने सईद इब्न अल-मुसय्यब से रिवायत की के अल्लाह के रसूल (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया: गिरवी रखी हुई चीज़ ज़ाया नहीं होती। इमाम मालिक ने कहा: इस की वज़ाहत जो हमारे नज़दीक है, और अल्लाह बेहतर जानता है, ये है के एक आदमी किसी चीज़ के बदले किसी को गिरवी देता है और कहता है के अगर मैं ने इतनी मुद्दत में तुम्हारा हक़ अदा न किया तो ये गिरवी तुम्हारी हो जाए गी। इमाम मालिक ने कहा: ये मामला दुरुस्त नहीं और हलाल नहीं, यही वो चीज़ है जिस से मना किया गया था।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1414]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1414" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے کہا کہ امام مالک نے ہمیں ابن شہاب سے، انہوں نے سعید بن مسیب سے روایت کی کہ اللہ کے رسول صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: گروی رکھی ہوئی چیز ضائع نہیں ہوتی۔ امام مالک نے کہا: اس کی وضاحت جو ہمارے نزدیک ہے، اور اللہ بہتر جانتا ہے، یہ ہے کہ ایک آدمی کسی چیز کے بدلے کسی کو گروی دیتا ہے اور کہتا ہے کہ اگر میں نے اتنی مدت میں تمہارا حق ادا نہ کیا تو یہ گروی تمہاری ہو جائے گی۔ امام مالک نے کہا: یہ معاملہ درست نہیں اور حلال نہیں، یہی وہ چیز ہے جس سے منع کیا گیا تھا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1414]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1414" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1414 ]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne kaha ke Imam Malik ne hamain Ibn Shihab se, unhon ne Saeed ibn al-Musayyab se riwayat ki ke Allah ke Rasool (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Girwi rakhi hui cheez zaya nahi hoti. Imam Malik ne kaha: Is ki wazahat jo hamare nazdeek hai, aur Allah behtar janta hai, ye hai ke ek aadmi kisi cheez ke badle kisi ko girwi deta hai aur kehta hai ke agar main ne itni muddat mein tumhara haq ada na kiya to ye girwi tumhari ho jaye gi. Imam Malik ne kaha: Ye mamla durust nahi aur halal nahi, yahi wo cheez hai jis se mana kiya gaya tha.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1414]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1414" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 460

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1416)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ مَنْ غَيَّرَ دِينَهُ فَاضْرِبُوا عُنُقَهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ وَمَعْنَى قَوْلِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِيمَا نُرَى - وَاللَّهُ أَعْلَمُ - ‏&quot;‏ مَنْ غَيَّرَ دِينَهُ فَاضْرِبُوا عُنُقَهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ أَنَّهُ مَنْ خَرَجَ مِنَ الإِسْلاَمِ إِلَى غَيْرِهِ مِثْلُ الزَّنَادِقَةِ وَأَشْبَاهِهِمْ فَإِنَّ أُولَئِكَ إِذَا ظُهِرَ عَلَيْهِمْ قُتِلُوا وَلَمْ يُسْتَتَابُوا لأَنَّهُ لاَ تُعْرَفُ تَوْبَتُهُمْ وَأَنَّهُمْ كَانُوا يُسِرُّونَ الْكُفْرَ وَيُعْلِنُونَ الإِسْلاَمَ فَلاَ أَرَى أَنْ يُسْتَتَابَ هَؤُلاَءِ وَلاَ يُقْبَلُ مِنْهُمْ قَوْلُهُمْ وَأَمَّا مَنْ خَرَجَ مِنَ الإِسْلاَمِ إِلَى غَيْرِهِ وَأَظْهَرَ ذَلِكَ فَإِنَّهُ يُسْتَتَابُ فَإِنْ تَابَ وَإِلاَّ قُتِلَ وَذَلِكَ لَوْ أَنَّ قَوْمًا كَانُوا عَلَى ذَلِكَ رَأَيْتُ أَنْ يُدْعَوْا إِلَى الإِسْلاَمِ وَيُسْتَتَابُوا فَإِنْ تَابُوا قُبِلَ ذَلِكَ مِنْهُمْ وَإِنْ لَمْ يَتُوبُوا قُتِلُوا وَلَمْ يُعْنَ بِذَلِكَ فِيمَا نُرَى وَاللَّهُ أَعْلَمُ مَنْ خَرَجَ مِنَ الْيَهُودِيَّةِ إِلَى النَّصْرَانِيَّةِ وَلاَ مِنَ النَّصْرَانِيَّةِ إِلَى الْيَهُودِيَّةِ وَلاَ مَنْ يُغَيِّرُ دِينَهُ مِنْ أَهْلِ الأَدْيَانِ كُلِّهَا إِلاَّ الإِسْلاَمَ فَمَنْ خَرَجَ مِنَ الإِسْلاَمِ إِلَى غَيْرِهِ وَأَظْهَرَ ذَلِكَ فَذَلِكَ الَّذِي عُنِيَ بِهِ وَاللَّهُ أَعْلَمُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1416]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1416" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;What is done in our community about someone who consumed an animal without the permission of its owner, is that he must pay its price on the day he consumed it. He is not obliged to replace it with a similar animal nor does he compensate the owner with any kind of animal. He must pay its price on the day it was consumed, and giving the value is more equitable in compensation for animals and goods.&quot; Yahya said that he heard Malik say about someone who consumes some food without the permission of its owner, &quot;He returns to the owner a like weight of the same kind of food. Food is in the position of gold and silver. Gold and silver are returned with gold and silver. The animal is not in the position of gold in that. What distinguishes between them is the sunna and the behaviour which is in force. Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;If a man is entrusted with some wealth and then trades with it for himself and makes a profit, the profit is his because he is responsible for the property until he returns it to its owner. &quot; Yahya related to me from Malik from Zayd ibn Aslam that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;If someone changes his deen - strike his neck!&quot; The meaning of the statement of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, in our opinion and Allah knows best, is that &quot;if someone changes his deen, strike his neck!&quot; refers to those who leave Islam for other than it - like the heretics and their like, about whom it is known. They are killed without being called to tawba because their tawba is not recognised. They were hiding their kufr and publishing their Islam, so I do not think that one calls such people to tawba, and one does not accept their word. As for the one who goes out of Islam to something else and divulges it, one calls him to tawba. If he does not turn in tawba, he is killed. If there are people in that situation, I think that one should call them to Islam and call them to tawba. If they turn in tawba, that is accepted from them. If they do not turn in tawba, they are killed. That does not refer as we see it, and Allah knows best, to those who come out of Judaism to Christianity or from Christianity to Judaism, nor to someone who changes his deen from the various forms of deen except for Islam. Whoever comes out of Islam to other than it and divulges that, that is the one who is referred to, and Allah knows best</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1416]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1416" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">ज़ैद बिन असलम से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया जो शख़्स अपना दीन बदल डाले (मुर्तद हो जाए) तो उसकी गर्दन मारो। मालिक ने कहा रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के इस फरमान का हमारे नज़दीक मतलब ये है के जो मुसलमान इस्लाम से बाहर हो जाए जैसे ज़नादिक़ा या उनकी मानिंद, तो जब मुसलमान उन पर ग़लबा पाएँ तो उन्हें क़त्ल कर दें। ये भी ज़रूरी नहीं के पहले उनसे तौबा करने को कहें क्योंकि उनकी तौबा का ऐतबार नहीं हो सकता, वो कुफ़्र को अपने दिल में रखते हैं और ज़ाहिर में अपने तईं मुसलमान कहते हैं। लेकिन अगर कोई मुसलमान शख़्स किसी शुब्हे की वजह से एलानिया दीन-ए-इस्लाम से फिर जाए, तो उस से तौबा करवाएँ (और जो शुबा हो उसे दूर करें), अगर तौबा करे तो बेहतर वरना क़त्ल किया जाए। और जो काफ़िर एक कुफ़्र के दीन को छोड़ कर दूसरा कुफ़्र का दीन इख़्तियार करे, मिसाल के तौर पर पहले यहूदी था फिर नस्रानी हो जाए, तो उसे क़त्ल नहीं करेंगे, बल्कि जो दीन-ए-इस्लाम को छोड़ कर और कोई दीन इख़्तियार करेगा उसी के लिए ये सज़ा है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1416]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1416" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">زید بن اسلم سے روایت ہے کہ فرمایا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے جو شخص اپنا دین بدل ڈالے تو اس کی گردن مارو۔ کہا مالک نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے یہ جو فرمایا جو شخص اپنا دین بدل ڈالے اس کی گردن مارو ہمارے نزدیک اس کے معنی یہ ہیں جو مسلمان اسلام سے باہر ہوجائیں جیسے زنادقہ یا ان کی مانند تو جب مسلمان ان پر غلبہ پائیں تو ان کو قتل کردیں یہ بھی ضروری نہیں کہ پہلے ان سے توبہ کرنے کو کہیں کیونکہ ان کی توبہ کا اعتبار نہیں ہوسکتا وہ کفر کو اپنے دل میں رکھتے ہیں اور ظاہر میں اپنے تئیں مسلمان کہتے ہیں لیکن اگر مسلمان شخص (کسی شبہ کی وجہ سے) علانیہ دین اسلام سے پھر جائے تو اس سے توبہ کرائیں (اور جو شبہ ہوا ہو اس کو دور کردیں) اگر توبہ کرے تو بہتر۔ ورنہ قتل کیا جائے اور جو کافر ایک کفر کے دین کو چھوڑ کر دوسرا کفر کا دین اختیار کرے مثلا پہلے یہودی تھا پھر نصرانی ہوجائے تو اس کو قتل نہ کریں گے بلکہ جو دین اسلام کو چھوڑ کر اور کوئی دین اختیار کرے گا اسی کے لئے یہ سزاہے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1416]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1416" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Zaid bin Aslam se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya jo shakhs apna deen badal dale (murtad ho jaye) toh uski gardan maro. Malik ne kaha Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke is farman ka hamare nazdeek matlab ye hai ke jo Musalman Islam se bahar ho jaye jaise Zanadiqa ya unki manind, toh jab Musalman un par ghalba payein toh unhein qatl kar dein. Ye bhi zaroori nahi ke pehle unse tauba karne ko kahein kyunke unki tauba ka aitbar nahi ho sakta, woh kufr ko apne dil mein rakhte hain aur zahir mein apne tayein Musalman kehte hain. Lekin agar koi Musalman shakhs kisi shubhe ki wajah se alaniya deen-e-Islam se phir jaye, toh us se tauba karwayein (aur jo shuba ho use door karein), agar tauba kare toh behtar warna qatl kiya jaye. Aur jo kafir ek kufr ke deen ko chhod kar dusra kufr ka deen ikhtiyar kare, misal ke taur par pehle Yahudi tha phir Nasrani ho jaye, toh use qatl nahi karenge, balki jo deen-e-Islam ko chhod kar aur koi deen ikhtiyar karega usi ke liye ye saza hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1416]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1416" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 461

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1418)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ سُهَيْلِ بْنِ أَبِي صَالِحٍ السَّمَّانِ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ سَعْدَ بْنَ عُبَادَةَ، قَالَ لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَرَأَيْتَ إِنْ وَجَدْتُ مَعَ امْرَأَتِي رَجُلاً أَأُمْهِلُهُ حَتَّى آتِيَ بِأَرْبَعَةِ شُهَدَاءَ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ نَعَمْ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1418]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1418" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Suhayl ibn Abi Salih as- Samman from his father from Abu Hurayra that Sad ibn Ubada said to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, &quot;What do you think if I find a man with my wife? Shall I grant him a respite until I bring four witnesses?&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, replied, &quot;Yes</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1418]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1418" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के सा&#x27;आद बिन उबड़ा ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से कहा अगर मैं अपनी औरत के साथ किसी मर्द को पौन तोह क्या मैं उसको मोहलत दूँ यहाँ तक के चार गवाह लॉन? फ़रमाया आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1418]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1418" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ سعد بن عبادہ نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے کہا اگر میں اپنی عورت کے ساتھ کسی مرد کو پاؤں کیا میں اس کو مہلت دوں یہاں تک کہ چار گواہ لاؤں فرمایا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے ہاں ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1418]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Huraira (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Sa&#x27;ad bin Ubada ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se kaha agar main apni aurat ke saath kisi mard ko paun toh kya main usko mohlat dun yahan tak ke chaar gawah laun? Farmaya Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne, Haan.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1418]</p>

## Narration 462

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1421)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">قَالَ يَحْيَى عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عُرْوَةَ بْنِ الزُّبَيْرِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّهَا قَالَتْ كَانَ عُتْبَةُ بْنُ أَبِي وَقَّاصٍ عَهِدَ إِلَى أَخِيهِ سَعْدِ بْنِ أَبِي وَقَّاصٍ أَنَّ ابْنَ وَلِيدَةِ زَمْعَةَ مِنِّي فَاقْبِضْهُ إِلَيْكَ ‏.‏ قَالَتْ فَلَمَّا كَانَ عَامُ الْفَتْحِ أَخَذَهُ سَعْدٌ وَقَالَ ابْنُ أَخِي قَدْ كَانَ عَهِدَ إِلَىَّ فِيهِ ‏.‏ فَقَامَ إِلَيْهِ عَبْدُ بْنُ زَمْعَةَ فَقَالَ أَخِي وَابْنُ وَلِيدَةِ أَبِي وُلِدَ عَلَى فِرَاشِهِ ‏.‏ فَتَسَاوَقَا إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ سَعْدٌ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ابْنُ أَخِي قَدْ كَانَ عَهِدَ إِلَىَّ فِيهِ ‏.‏ وَقَالَ عَبْدُ بْنُ زَمْعَةَ أَخِي وَابْنُ وَلِيدَةِ أَبِي وُلِدَ عَلَى فِرَاشِهِ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ هُوَ لَكَ يَا عَبْدُ بْنَ زَمْعَةَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ ثُمَّ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ الْوَلَدُ لِلْفِرَاشِ وَلِلْعَاهِرِ الْحَجَرُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ ثُمَّ قَالَ لِسَوْدَةَ بِنْتِ زَمْعَةَ ‏&quot;‏ احْتَجِبِي مِنْهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ لِمَا رَأَى مِنْ شَبَهِهِ بِعُتْبَةَ بْنِ أَبِي وَقَّاصٍ قَالَتْ فَمَا رَآهَا حَتَّى لَقِيَ اللَّهَ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1421]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1421" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Urwa ibn az-Zubayr that A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;&#x27;Utba ibn Abi Waqqas disclosed to his brother, Sad ibn Abi Waqqas, that he was the father of the son of the slave-girl of Zama, and made him promise to look after him (after his death). In the year of the conquest, Sad took him and said, &#x27;He is the son of my brother. He covenanted with me about him.&#x27; Abd ibn Zama stood up and said, &#x27;He is my brother and the son of my father&#x27;s slave-girl. He was born on his bed.&#x27; They went to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace. Sad said, &#x27;Messenger of Allah! He is the son of my brother, he made a covenant with me about him.&#x27; Abd ibn Zama said, &#x27;He is my brother and the son of my father&#x27;s slave-girl and was born on my father&#x27;s bed.&#x27; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;He is yours, Abd ibn Zama.&#x27; Then the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;A child belongs to the household (where he was born) and the adulterer is stoned.&#x27; Then he told Sawda bint Zama, &#x27;Veil yourself from him,&#x27; since he saw in him a resemblance to Utba ibn Abi Waqqas.&quot; A&#x27;isha added, &quot;He did not see her until he met Allah, the Mighty, the Majestic</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1421]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1421" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत आयशा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) से रिवायत है के उत्बाह बिन अबी वक़्क़ास ने मरते वक़्त अपने भाई साअद बिन अबी वक़्क़ास से कहा के ज़माअह की लौंडी का लड़का मेरे नुत्फ़ा से है तो उसको अपने पास रखियो। जब मक्का फ़तह हुआ तो साअद ने उस लड़के को ले लिया और कहा ये मेरे भाई का बेटा है, उसने वसीयत की थी इसके लेने की। अब्द-ए-ज़माअह ने कहा ये लड़का मेरा भाई है, मेरे बाप की लौंडी का बेटा है। दोनों ने झगड़ा किया तो रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास गए। साअद ने कहा या रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ये बेटा है मेरे भाई का, उसने मुझे वसीयत की थी और मेरे बाप की लौंडी से पैदा हुआ है। रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने अब्द-ए-ज़माअह से फरमाया के ये लड़का तेरा है, फिर फरमाया लड़का माँ के ख़ावंद या मालिक का होता है और ज़िना करने वाले के लिए पत्थर (सज़ा) है। फिर सौदा बिंत ज़माअह से कहा के तू इस लड़के से पर्दा किया कर क्योंकि वो लड़का मुशाबाह था उत्बाह बिन अबी वक़्क़ास के, सो उस लड़के ने न देखा सौदा को यहाँ तक के उसका इंतेक़ाल हो गया।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1421]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1421" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت عائشہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہا سے روایت ہے کہ عتبہ بن ابی وقاص نے مرتے وقت اپنے بھائی سعد بن ابی وقاص سے کہا کہ زمعہ کی لونڈی کا لڑکا میرے نطفہ سے ہے تو اس کو اپنے پاس رکھیوں تو جب مکہ فتح ہوا تو سعد نے اس لڑکے کو لے لیا اور کہا میرے بھائی کا بیٹا ہے اس نے وصیت کی تھی اس کے لینے کی عبد زمعہ نے کہا یہ لڑکا میرا بھائی ہے میرے باپ کی لونڈی کا بیٹا ہے دونوں نے جھگڑا کیا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے پاس سعد نے کہا یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم یہ بیٹا ہے میرے بھائی کا اس نے مجھے وصیت کی تھی اور میرے باپ کی لونڈی سے پیدا ہوا ہے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا عبد زمعہ سے کہ یہ لڑکا تیرا ہے پھر فرمایا لڑکا ماں کے خاوند یا مالک کا ہوتا ہے اور زنا کرنے والے کے لئے پتھر ہیں پھر سودہ بنت زمعہ سے کہا کہ تو اس لڑکے سے پردہ کیا کر کیونکہ وہ لڑکا مشابہ تھا عتبہ بن ابی وقاص کے سو اس لڑکے نے نہ دیکھا سودہ کر یہاں تک کہ انتقال ہوا اس کا ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1421]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1421" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Ayesha (Radi Allahu Anha) se riwayat hai ke Utbah bin Abi Waqqas ne marte waqt apne bhai Sa&#x27;ad bin Abi Waqqas se kaha ke Zama&#x27;ah ki laundi ka ladka mere nutfa se hai toh usko apne paas rakhiyo. Jab Makkah fatah hua toh Sa&#x27;ad ne us ladke ko le liya aur kaha ye mere bhai ka beta hai, usne wasiyat ki thi iske lene ki. Abd-e-Zama&#x27;ah ne kaha ye ladka mera bhai hai, mere baap ki laundi ka beta hai. Dono ne jhagda kiya toh Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas gaye. Sa&#x27;ad ne kaha ya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ye beta hai mere bhai ka, usne mujhe wasiyat ki thi aur mere baap ki laundi se paida hua hai. Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne Abd-e-Zama&#x27;ah se farmaya ke ye ladka tera hai, phir farmaya ladka maa ke khawand ya maalik ka hota hai aur zina karne wale ke liye patthar (saza) hai. Phir Saudah bint Zama&#x27;ah se kaha ke tu is ladke se parda kiya kar kyunke wo ladka mushabah tha Utbah bin Abi Waqqas ke, so us ladke ne na dekha Saudah ko yahan tak ke uska inteqal ho gaya.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1421]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1421" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 463

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1427)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مَنْ أَحْيَا أَرْضًا مَيِّتَةً فَهِيَ لَهُ وَلَيْسَ لِعِرْقٍ ظَالِمٍ حَقٌّ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1427]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1427" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1427</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1427 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related from Malik from Hisham ibn Urwa from his father that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;If anyone revives dead land, it belongs to him, and the unjust root has no right.&quot; Malik explained, &quot;The unjust root is whatever is taken, or planted without right</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1427]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1427" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">उरवह बिन ज़ुबैर से रिवायत है के फ़रमाया रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने आबाद करे वो उसी की है. जो शख्स ज़ुल्म से वहां कुछ तसर्रुफ़ करे उसको कुछ हक़ नहीं है.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1427]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1427" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عروہ بن زبیر سے روایت ہے کہ فرمایا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے جو شخص بنجر زمین کو آباد کرے وہ اسی کی ہے جو شخص ظلم سے وہاں کچھ تصرف کرے اس کو کچھ حق نہیں ہے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1427]</p>
