## Narration 464

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1429)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ حَزْمٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ فِي سَيْلِ مَهْزُورٍ وَمُذَيْنِبٍ ‏ &quot;‏ يُمْسَكُ حَتَّى الْكَعْبَيْنِ ثُمَّ يُرْسِلُ الأَعْلَى عَلَى الأَسْفَلِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1429]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1429" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1429</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1429 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abdullah ibn Abi Bakr ibn Muhammad ibn Amr ibn Hazm that he heard that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said about the flood-channels of Mahzur and Mudhaynib (in Madina), &quot;Dam them systematically, so that the water is diverted into each property in turn up to ankle level, starting upstream</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1429]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1429" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1429</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1429 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्ला बिन अबी बक्र से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया दो नालों में से, एक का नाम महरोज़ था और दूसरे का नाम मुज़ानिब, के जिसका बाग़ नाले के मुत्तसिल है वो अपने बाग़ में टखनों-टखनों पानी भर के फिर दूसरे के बाग़ में पानी छोड़ दे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1429]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1429" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن ابی بکر سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا دونالوں میں ایک کا نام مہروز تھا اور دوسرے کا نام مذینیب کہ جس کا باغ نالہ کے متصل ہے وہ اپنے باغ میں ٹخنوں ٹخنوں پانی بھر کے پھر دوسرے کے باغ میں پانی چھوڑ دے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1429]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1429" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Abi Bakr se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya do nalon mein se, ek ka naam Mahroz tha aur dusre ka naam Muzanib, ke jiska bagh nale ke muttasil hai wo apne bagh mein takhno-takhno pani bhar ke phir dusre ke bagh mein pani chhod de.</p>

## Narration 465

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1430)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، عَنِ الأَعْرَجِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ يُمْنَعُ فَضْلُ الْمَاءِ لِيُمْنَعَ بِهِ الْكَلأُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1430]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1430" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1430</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1430 ]</p>

</details>

<details>

## Narration 466

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1431)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ أَبِي الرِّجَالِ، مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ عَنْ أُمِّهِ، عَمْرَةَ بِنْتِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ أَنَّهَا أَخْبَرَتْهُ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ يُمْنَعُ نَقْعُ بِئْرٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1431]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1431" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1431</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1431 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Abu&#x27;r-Rijal Muhammad ibn Abd ar-Rahman from his mother Amra bint Abd ar-Rahman that she informed him that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Do not withhold the surplus water of a well from people</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1431]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1431" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1431]</p>

## Narration 467

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1432)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ يَحْيَى الْمَازِنِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ ضَرَرَ وَلاَ ضِرَارَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1432]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1432" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1432" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 468

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1433)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنِ الأَعْرَجِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ يَمْنَعُ أَحَدُكُمْ جَارَهُ خَشَبَةً يَغْرِزُهَا فى جِدَارِهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ ثُمَّ يَقُولُ أَبُو هُرَيْرَةَ مَا لِي أَرَاكُمْ عَنْهَا مُعْرِضِينَ وَاللَّهِ لأَرْمِيَنَّ بِهَا بَيْنَ أَكْتَافِكُمْ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1433]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1433" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1433</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1433 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Ibn Shihab from al-Araj from Abu Hurayra that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;No one should prevent his neighbour from fixing a wooden peg in his wall.&quot; Then Abu Hurayra said, &quot;Why do I see you turning away from it? By Allah! I shall keep on at you about it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1433]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1433" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के फ़रमाया रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने: मन करते तुम में से कोई अपने हमसाया को लकड़ी गाड़ने से अपनी दीवार में. फिर अबू हुरैरह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते थे: क्या वजह है के तुम इस हदीथ को मुतवज्जा हो कर नहीं सुनते? क़सम अल्लाह की</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1433]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1433" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ فرمایا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے منع کرتے تم میں سے کوئی اپنے ہمسایہ کو لکڑی گاڑنے سے اپنی دیوار میں پھر ابوہریرہ کہتے تھے کیا وجہ ہے کہ تم اس حدیث کو متوجہ ہو کر نہیں سنتے قسم اللہ کی میں اس کو خوب مشہور کروں گا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1433]</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke farmaya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne: Mana karte tum mein se koi apne hamsaya ko lakdi gaadne se apni deewar mein. Phir Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) kehte the: Kya wajah hai ke tum is hadith ko mutawajja ho kar nahi sunte? Qasam Allah ki, main is ko khoob mashhoor karunga.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1433]</p>

## Narration 469

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1436)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ ثَوْرِ بْنِ زَيْدٍ الدِّيلِيِّ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ بَلَغَنِي أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ أَيُّمَا دَارٍ أَوْ أَرْضٍ قُسِمَتْ فِي الْجَاهِلِيَّةِ فَهِيَ عَلَى قَسْمِ الْجَاهِلِيَّةِ وَأَيُّمَا دَارٍ أَوْ أَرْضٍ أَدْرَكَهَا الإِسْلاَمُ وَلَمْ تُقْسَمْ فَهِيَ عَلَى قَسْمِ الإِسْلاَمِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى سَمِعْتُ مَالِكًا يَقُولُ فِيمَنْ هَلَكَ وَتَرَكَ أَمْوَالاً بِالْعَالِيَةِ وَالسَّافِلَةِ إِنَّ الْبَعْلَ لاَ يُقْسَمُ مَعَ النَّضْحِ إِلاَّ أَنْ يَرْضَى أَهْلُهُ بِذَلِكَ وَإِنَّ الْبَعْلَ يُقْسَمُ مَعَ الْعَيْنِ إِذَا كَانَ يُشْبِهُهَا وَأَنَّ الأَمْوَالَ إِذَا كَانَتْ بِأَرْضٍ وَاحِدَةٍ الَّذِي بَيْنَهُمَا مُتَقَارِبٌ أَنَّهُ يُقَامُ كُلُّ مَالٍ مِنْهَا ثُمَّ يُقْسَمُ بَيْنَهُمْ وَالْمَسَاكِنُ وَالدُّورُ بِهَذِهِ الْمَنْزِلَةِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1436]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1436" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1436</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1436 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Thawr ibn Zayd ad-Dili said, &quot;I heard that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;A house or land that has been divided in the Jahiliyya, it is according to the division of the Jahiliyya. A house or land which has not been divided before the coming of Islam is divided according to Islam</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1436]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1436" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1436</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1436 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">थौर बिन जैद वाइली से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: जो ज़मीन या मकान जाहिलियत के ज़माने में तक़सीम हो चुका है वो इसी तौर पर रहेगा। अलबता जो मकान या ज़मीन इस्लाम के ज़माने तक तक़सीम नहीं हुई, तो वो इस्लाम के क़ायदों के मुवाफ़िक़ तक़सीम होगी। कहा मालिक ने: अगर एक शख़्स मर जाए और बारानी और चाही ज़मीनें छोड़ जाए तो बारानी को चाही के साथ मिला कर तक़सीम न करेंगे बल्कि जुदा जुदा तक़सीम करेंगे (क्यूँके बारानी का लगान दसवां हिस्सा और चाही का बीसवां हिस्सा पैदावार का है), मगर जब सब शरीक मिला कर तक़सीम करने पर राज़ी हो जाएं तो मिला कर तक़सीम कर देंगे। अलबता बारानी और ज़ेर-ए-तलब या क रीज़ को मिला कर तक़सीम कर देंगे (क्यूँके उनका धारा एक है, यानी दोनों क़िस्म की ज़मीनों का लगान पैदावार का दसवां हिस्सा है)। इसी तरह अगर किसी क़िस्म के माल हो एक ही जगह और एक दूसरे के मुशाबा हो, तो हर एक माल की क़ीमत लगा कर एक साथ तक़सीम कर देंगे, मकानों और घरों का भी यही हुक्म है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1436]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1436" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ثور بن زید ویلی سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا جو زمین یا مکان جاہلیت کے زمانے میں تقسیم ہو چکا ہے وہ اسی طور پر رہیگا البتہ جو مکان یا زمین اسلام کے زمانے تک تقسیم نہیں ہوئی تو وہ اسلام کے قاعدوں کے موافق تقسیم ہوگی ۔ کہا مالک نے اگر ایک شخص مرجائے اور بارانی اور چاہی زمینیں چھوڑ جائے تو بارانی کو چاہی کے ساتھ ملا کر تقسیم نہ کریں گے بلکہ جدا جداتقسیم کریں گے۔ (کیونکہ بارانی کا لگان دسواں حصہ اور چاہی کا بیسواں حصہ پیداوار کا) مگر جب سب شریک ملا کر تقسیم کرنے پر راضی ہوجائیں تو ملا کر تقسیم کردیں گے البتہ بارانی اور زیر تالاب یا کاریز کو ملا کر تقسیم کردیں گے ۔ (کیونکہ ان کا دھارا ایک ہے یعنی دونوں قسموں کی زمینوں کا لگان پیداوار کا دسواں حصہ ہے اسی طرح اگر کسی قسم کے مال ہوں ایک ہی جگہ اور ایک دوسرے کے مشابہ ہوں تو ہر ایک مال کی قیمت لگا کر ایک ساتھ تقسیم کردیں گے مکانوں اور گھروں کا بھی یہی حکم ہے۔)</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1436]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1436" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Thawr bin Zaid Waili se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Jo zameen ya makan jahiliyat ke zamane mein taqseem ho chuka hai woh isi taur par rahega. Albata jo makan ya zameen Islam ke zamane tak taqseem nahi hui, to woh Islam ke qaido ke muwafiq taqseem hogi. Kaha Malik ne: Agar ek shakhs marjaye aur barani aur chahi zameenein chhod jaye to barani ko chahi ke saath mila kar taqseem na karenge balki juda juda taqseem karenge (kyunke barani ka lagan daswan hissa aur chahi ka beeswan hissa paidawar ka hai), magar jab sab shareek mila kar taqseem karne par raazi hojayein to mila kar taqseem kardenge. Albata barani aur zer-e-talab ya kareez ko mila kar taqseem kardenge (kyunke unka dhara ek hai, yani dono qismo ki zameeno ka lagan paidawar ka daswan hissa hai). Isi tarah agar kisi qism ke maal ho ek hi jagah aur ek dusre ke mushaba ho, to har ek maal ki qeemat laga kar ek saath taqseem kardenge, makanon aur gharon ka bhi yahi hukum hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1436]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1436" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 470

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1439)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ حُمَيْدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ عَوْفٍ، وَعَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ النُّعْمَانِ بْنِ بَشِيرٍ، أَنَّهُمَا حَدَّثَاهُ عَنِ النُّعْمَانِ بْنِ بَشِيرٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ إِنَّ أَبَاهُ بَشِيرًا أَتَى بِهِ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ إِنِّي نَحَلْتُ ابْنِي هَذَا غُلاَمًا كَانَ لِي ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ أَكُلَّ وَلَدِكَ نَحَلْتَهُ مِثْلَ هَذَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لاَ ‏.‏ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ فَارْتَجِعْهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1439]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1439" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1439</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1439 ]</p>

</details>

<details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;What is done in our community about injury to a domestic animal, is that the one who injures it must pay the amount by which he has diminished the animal&#x27;s price.&quot; Yahya said that he heard Malik speak about a camel who attacked a man and he feared for himself and killed it or hamstrung it. He said, &quot;If he has a clear proof that it was heading for him and had attacked him, there are no damages against him. If there is no clear proof except his word, he is responsible for the camel.&quot; Yahya related that he heard Malik say that if a man gave a washer a garment to dye and he dyed it, and then the owner of the garment said, &quot;I did not order you to use this dye,&quot; and the washer protested that he had done so, then the washer was to be believed. It was the same with the tailor and the gold-smith. They took an oath about it unless they produced something they would not normally have been employed to do. In that situation their statement was not allowed and the owner of the garment had to take an oath . If he rejected it and refused to swear, then the dyer was made to take an oath. Yahya said, &quot;I heard Malik speak about a dyer who was given a garment and he made a mistake and gave it to another man and the one to whom he gave it wore it. He said, &#x27;The one who wore it has no damages against him, and the washer pays damages to the owner of the garment. That is when the man wears the garment which was given him without recognizing that it is not his. If he wears it knowing that it is not his garment, he is responsible for it.&#x27; &quot; Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;What is done in our community about a man who refers a creditor to another man for the debt he owes him is that if the one referred to goes bankrupt or dies, and does not leave enough to pay the debt, then the creditor has nothing against the one who referred him and the debt does not return to the first party.&quot; Malik said, &quot;This is the way of doing things about which there is no dispute in our community.&quot; Malik said, &quot;If a man has his debt to somebody taken on for him by another man and then the man who took it on dies or goes bankrupt, then whatever was taken on by him returns to the first debtor.&quot; Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;If a man buys a garment which has a defect, a burn or something else, which the seller knows about and that is testified against him or he confirms it, and the man who has bought it causes a new tear which decreases the price of the garment, and then he learns about the original defect, he can return it to the seller and he is not liable for his tearing it. &quot;If a man buys a garment which has a defect of a burn or flaw, and the one who sold it to him claims that he did not know about it, and the buyer has cut the garment or dyed it, then the buyer has an option . If he wishes, he can have a reduction according to what the burn or flaw detracts from the price of the garment and he can keep the garment, or if he wishes to pay damages for what the cutting or dyeing has decreased of the price of the garment and return it, he can do so. &quot;If the buyer has dyed the garment with a dye which increases the value, the buyer has an option. If he wishes, he has a reduction from the price of the garment according to what the defect diminishes or if he wishes to become a partner with the one who sold the garment he does so. The price of the garment with a burn or flaw is looked at. If the price is ten dirhams, and the amount by which the dyeing increased the value is five dirhams, then they are partners in the garment, each according to his share. In this reckoning is the amount by which the dyeing increases the price of the garment.&quot; Yahya related to us from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Humayd ibn Abd ar-Rah man ibn Awf and from Muhammad ibn an-Numan ibn Bashir that they related to him that an-Numan ibn Bashir said that his father Bashir brought him to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and said, &quot;I have given this son of mine one of my slaves.&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Have you given each of your children the same as this?&quot; He said, &quot;No.&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Then take the slave back</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1439]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1439" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">नोमान बिन बशीर से रिवायत है के मेरे बाप मुझ को रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास लेकर आये और कहा: या रसूल अल्लाह! मैं ने इस बेटे को अपना एक घुलम हीबा किया है. रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: क्या सब बेटों को तूने ऐसा ही घुलम दिया? बोलै: नहीं. तो आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: रुजू कर हिबा से.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1439]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1439" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">نعمان بن بشیر سے رویات ہے کہ میرے باپ مجھ کو رسول اللہ کے پاس لے کر آئے اور کہا یا رسول اللہ میں نے اس بیٹے کو اپنا ایک غلام ہبہ کیا ہے رسول اللہ نے فرمایا کیا سب بیٹوں کو تو نے ایسا ہی غلام دیا بولا نہیں تو آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا رجوع کر ہبہ سے</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1439]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1439" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Numan bin Bashir se riwayat hai ke mere baap mujh ko Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas lekar aaye aur kaha: Ya Rasool Allah! Main ne is bete ko apna ek ghulam hiba kiya hai. Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Kya sab beton ko tune aisa hi ghulam diya? Bola: Nahi. To aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Ruju kar hiba se.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1439]</p>

## Narration 471

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1443)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ عَوْفٍ، عَنْ جَابِرِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ الأَنْصَارِيِّ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ أَيُّمَا رَجُلٍ أُعْمِرَ عُمْرَى لَهُ وَلِعَقِبِهِ فَإِنَّهَا لِلَّذِي يُعْطَاهَا لاَ تَرْجِعُ إِلَى الَّذِي أَعْطَاهَا أَبَدًا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ لأَنَّهُ أَعْطَى عَطَاءً وَقَعَتْ فِيهِ الْمَوَارِيثُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1443]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1443" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;The way of doing things in our community about which there is no dispute, is that if a man gives sadaqa to his son - sadaqa which the son takes possession of or which is in the father&#x27;s keeping and the father has had his sadaqa witnessed, he cannot take back any of it because he cannot reclaim any sadaqa.&quot; Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;The generally agreed-on way of doing things in our community in the case of someone who gives his son a gift or grants him a gift which is not sadaqa is that he can take it back as long as the child does not start a debt, which people claim from him, and which they trust him for on the strength of the gift his father has given him. The father cannot take back anything from the gift after debts are started against it. &quot;If a man gives his son or daughter something and a woman marries the man, and she only marries him for the wealth and the property which his father has given him and so the father wants to take that back, or, if a man marries a woman whose father has given her a gift and he marries her with an increased bride-price because of the wealth and property that her father has given, then the father says, &#x27;I will take that back,&#x27; then the father cannot take back any of that from the son or daughter if it is as I have described to you.&quot; Malik related to me from Ibn Shihab from Abu Salama ibn Abd ar- Rahman ibn Awf from Jabir ibn Abdullah al-Ansari that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;If someone is given a life pension, for him and his posterity, it belongs to the person to whom it has been given. It never reverts to the one who gave it because he gave a gift and the rules of inheritance apply to it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1443]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1443" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">जाबिर बिन अब्दुल्लाह अंसारी (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया, जो शख्स किसी को उमरा (तोहफे के तौर पर जिंदगी भर के लिए देना) दे, उसके वास्ते और उसके वारिसों के वास्ते, तोह फिर वोह उमरा उसी का हो जाता है, देने वाले को फिर नहीं मिल सकता।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1443]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">جابر بن عبداللہ انصاری سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا جو شخص کسی کو عمریٰ دے اس کے واسطے اور اس کے وارثوں کے واسطے تو پھر وہ عمرہ اس کا ہو جاتا ہے دینے ولاے کو پھر نہیں مل سکتا ۔</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Jabir bin Abdullah Ansari (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya, jo shakhs kisi ko Umra (tohfe ke taur par zindagi bhar ke liye dena) de, uske waste aur uske warison ke waste, toh phir woh umra usi ka ho jata hai, dene wale ko phir nahi mil sakta.</p>

## Narration 472

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1446)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ رَبِيعَةَ بْنِ أَبِي عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ يَزِيدَ، مَوْلَى الْمُنْبَعِثِ عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ خَالِدٍ الْجُهَنِيِّ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ جَاءَ رَجُلٌ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَسَأَلَهُ عَنِ اللُّقَطَةِ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ اعْرِفْ عِفَاصَهَا وَوِكَاءَهَا ثُمَّ عَرِّفْهَا سَنَةً فَإِنْ جَاءَ صَاحِبُهَا وَإِلاَّ فَشَأْنَكَ بِهَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ فَضَالَّةُ الْغَنَمِ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ هِيَ لَكَ أَوْ لأَخِيكَ أَوْ لِلذِّئْبِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ فَضَالَّةُ الإِبِلِ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ مَا لَكَ وَلَهَا مَعَهَا سِقَاؤُهَا وَحِذَاؤُهَا تَرِدُ الْمَاءَ وَتَأْكُلُ الشَّجَرَ حَتَّى يَلْقَاهَا رَبُّهَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1446]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1446" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Rabia ibn Abi Abd ar-Rahman from Yazid, the mawla of al-Munbaith that Zayd ibn Khalid al-Juhani said, &quot;A man came to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and asked him about finds. He said, &#x27;Memorize the characteristics of the object found, then publicise it for a year. If the owner comes, give it to him. If not, then it is your business.&#x27; He said, &#x27;What about lost sheep, Messenger of Allah?&#x27; He said, &#x27;They are yours, your brother&#x27;s or the wolf&#x27;s.&#x27; He said, &#x27;And the lost camel?&#x27; He said, &#x27;It&#x27;s none of your concern. It has its water and its feet. It will reach water and eat trees until its owner finds it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1446]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1446" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">जैद बिन खालिद जुहानी (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के एक शख्स आया रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास और पूछा आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से लुक्ता (गिरी हुई चीज़) के बारे में। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया, पहचान रख तरफ उसके लुक्ते की, ख्वाह चमड़े में हो या कपड़े में, और पहचान रख बंधन उसका, फिर एक बरस तक लोगों से उसका हाल कहा कर, अगर उसका मालिक मिल जाए तोह उसके दे दे, नहीं तोह ले ले। फिर उसने कहा, अगर कोई बकरी बहकी भटकी मिल जाए या रसूल अल्लाह? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया, बकरी तेरे काम में आएगी या तेरे भाई के, नहीं तोह भेड़िया खा जाएगा। फिर उस शख्स ने कहा, अगर ऊंट भूला मिले? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया, ऊंट से तुझे क्या काम? वोह तोह अपने साथ अपना पानी रखता है और मौज़े (पैर) रखता है, जहां उसको पानी मिल जाता है पी लेता है, जो दरख्त मिलता है खा लेता है यहां तक के मालिक उसका उसको पा लेता है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1446]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1446" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">زید بن خالد جہنی سے روایت ہے کہ ایک شخص آیا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے پاس اور پوچھا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے لقطہ کو فرمایا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے پہچان رکھ طرف اس کا لقطہ ہو خواہ چمڑے میں ہو یا کپڑے میں ہو پاور پہچان رکھ بندھن اس کا پھر ایک برس تک لوگوں سے اس کا حال کہا کر اگر اس کا مالک مل جائے تو اس کے دے دے نہیں تو لے لے پھر اس نے کہا گر کوئی بکری بہکی بھٹکی مل جائے یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا بکری تیرے کام میں آئے گی یا تیرے بھائی کے نہیں تو بھیڑیا کھا جائے گا پھر اس شخص نے کہا اگر اونٹ بھولا ملے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا اونٹ سے تجھے کیا کام وہ تو اپنے ساتھ اپنا پانی رکھتا ہے اور موزے رکھتا ہے جہاں اس کو پانی مل جاتا ہے پی لیتا ہے جو درخت ملتا ہے کھالیتا ہے یہاں تک کہ مالک اس کا اس کو پا لیاتا ہے</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1446]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1446" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Zaid bin Khalid Juhani (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke ek shakhs aaya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas aur pucha Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se luqta (giri hui cheez) ke bare mein. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya, pehchan rakh taraf uske luqte ki, khwah chamde mein ho ya kapde mein, aur pehchan rakh bandhan uska, phir ek baras tak logon se uska haal kaha kar, agar uska malik mil jaye toh uske de de, nahi toh le le. Phir usne kaha, agar koi bakri behki bhatki mil jaye ya Rasool Allah? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya, bakri tere kaam mein aayegi ya tere bhai ke, nahi toh bhediya kha jayega. Phir us shakhs ne kaha, agar oonth bhoola mile? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya, oonth se tujhe kya kaam? Woh toh apne saath apna pani rakhta hai aur mouze (pair) rakhta hai, jahan usko pani mil jata hai pi leta hai, jo darakht milta hai kha leta hai yahan tak ke malik uska usko pa leta hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1446]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1446" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 473

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1453)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّ رَجُلاً قَالَ لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِنَّ أُمِّي افْتُلِتَتْ نَفْسُهَا وَأُرَاهَا لَوْ تَكَلَّمَتْ تَصَدَّقَتْ أَفَأَتَصَدَّقُ عَنْهَا فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ نَعَمْ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1453]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1453" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1453</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1453 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Hisham ibn Urwa from his father from A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, that a man said to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, &quot;My mother died suddenly, and I think that had she spoken, she would have given sadaqa. Shall I give sadaqa for her?&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Yes</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1453]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1453" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मालिक ने हिशाम इब्न उरवा से, उन्होंने अपने वालिद से, उन्होंने आइशा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) से रिवायत की के एक शख्श ने नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से पूछा: मेरी वालिदा अचानक फौत हो गईं, मेरा ख्याल है के अगर वो बोलतिं तो सदका करतीं, क्या मैं उन की तरफ से सदका कर सकता हूं? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया: हां।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1453]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1453" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">امام مالک نے ہشام بن عروہ سے، انہوں نے اپنے والد سے، انہوں نے عائشہ رضی اللہ عنہا سے روایت کی کہ ایک شخص نے نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے پوچھا: میری والدہ اچانک فوت ہو گئیں، میرا خیال ہے کہ اگر وہ بولتیں تو صدقہ کرتیں، کیا میں ان کی طرف سے صدقہ کر سکتا ہوں؟ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: ہاں۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1453]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1453" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="ltr">Imam Malik ne Hisham ibn Urwa se, unhon ne apne walid se, unhon ne A&#x27;isha (R.A) se riwayat ki ke ek shakhs ne Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se pucha: Meri walida achanak fot ho gayin, mera khayal hai ke agar wo boltin to sadaqa kartin, kya main un ki taraf se sadaqa kar sakta hoon? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Haan.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1453]</p>

## Narration 474

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1454)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ رَجُلاً، مِنَ الأَنْصَارِ مِنْ بَنِي الْحَارِثِ بْنِ الْخَزْرَجِ تَصَدَّقَ عَلَى أَبَوَيْهِ بِصَدَقَةٍ فَهَلَكَا فَوَرِثَ ابْنُهُمَا الْمَالَ وَهُوَ نَخْلٌ فَسَأَلَ عَنْ ذَلِكَ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ قَدْ أُجِرْتَ فِي صَدَقَتِكَ وَخُذْهَا بِمِيرَاثِكَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1454]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1454" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1454</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1454 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me that he heard that a man of the Ansar from the tribe of Banu al-Harith ibn al-Khazraj, gave sadaqa to his parents and then they died. Their son inherited the property he had given them and it was palm-trees. He asked the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, about it and he said, &quot;You are rewarded for your sadaqa, and take it as your inheritance</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1454]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1454" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1454</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1454 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत आइशा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) से रिवायत है के एक शख़्स ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से कहा के मेरी माँ का दम यकायक निकल गया, अगर बात करने पाती तो ज़रूर सदक़ा करती, क्या मैं उसकी तरफ़ से सदक़ा करूँ? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया हाँ। एक अंसारी शख़्स ने अपने वालिदैन को खजूर के दरख़्त सदक़ा में दिए, फिर वालिदैन मर गए तो वही शख़्स उसका वारिस हुआ। उसने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से बयान किया, आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया तुझे सदक़े का सवाब हुआ, अब मीरास में इसको ले ले।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1454]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1454" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1454 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت عائشہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہا سے روایت ہے کہ ایک شخص نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے کہا کہ میرے ماں کا دم یکایک نکل گیا اگر بات کرنے پاتی تو ضرور صدقہ کرتی کیا میں اس کی طرف سے صدقہ کروں آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا ہاں ۔ ایک شخص انصاری نے اپنے والدین کو کھجور کے درخت صدقہ میں دئیے پھر والدین مر گئے تو وہی شخص اس کا وارث ہو اس نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے بیان کیا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا اتجھے صدقہ کا ثواب ہوا اب میراث میں اس کو لے لے</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1454]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1454" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1454 ]</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Aisha (Radi Allahu Anha) se riwayat hai ke ek shakhs ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se kaha ke meri maa ka dum yakayak nikal gaya, agar baat karne paati to zaroor sadaqa karti, kya main uski taraf se sadaqa karoon? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya haan. Ek Ansari shakhs ne apne walidain ko khajoor ke darakht sadaqa mein diye, phir walidain mar gaye to wahi shakhs uska waris hua. Usne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se bayan kiya, Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya tujhe sadaqe ka sawab hua, ab miras mein isko le le.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1454]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1454" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 475

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1455)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 37</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مَا حَقُّ امْرِئٍ مُسْلِمٍ لَهُ شَىْءٌ يُوصَى فِيهِ يَبِيتُ لَيْلَتَيْنِ إِلاَّ وَوَصِيَّتُهُ عِنْدَهُ مَكْتُوبَةٌ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1455]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1455" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1455</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1455 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Nafi from Abdullah ibn Umar that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;It is the duty of a muslim man who has something to be given as a bequest not to spend two nights without writing a will about it.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed-on way of doing things in our community is that when the testator writes something in health or illness as a bequest, and it has freeing slaves or things other than that in it, he can alter it in any way he chooses, until he is on his deathbed. If he prefers to abandon a bequest or change it, he can do so unless he has made a slave mudabbar (to be freed after his death). If he has made him mudabbar, there is no way to change what he has made mudabbar. He is allowed to change his testament because the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;It is the duty of a muslim man who has something to be given as a bequest not to spend two nights without writing a will about it.&quot; Malik explained, &quot;Had the testator not been able to change his will nor what was mentioned in it about freeing slaves, each testator might withhold making bequests from his property, whether in freeing slaves or other than it. A man gives a bequest in his health and in his travelling.&quot; (i.e. he does not wait till his death bed ) . Malik summed up, &quot;The way of doing things in our community about which there is no dispute is that he can change whatever he likes of that except for the mudabbar</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1455]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1455" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया है आदमी को जिसके पास चीज़ या मामला ऐसा हो जिसमें वसीयत करना ज़रूरी हो और वो दो रातें गुज़ारे बग़ैर वसीयत लिखे हुए. मलिक ने कहा अगर मौसी अपनी वसीयत के बदलने पर क़ादिर न होता तो चाहिए था के हर वसीयत करने वाले का माल उसके इख़्तियार से निकल कर रुका रहता तदबीर के. मलिक ने कहा हमारे नज़दीक हर वसीयत को बदल सकता है सिवाए तद्बीर के.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1455]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1455" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا نہیں لائق ہے آدمی کو جس کے پاس چیز یا معاملہ ایسا ہو جس میں وصیت کرنا ضروری ہو اور وہ دو راتیں گزارے بغیر وصیت لکھے ہؤے کہا مالک نے اگر موصی اپنی وصیت کے بدلنے پر قادر نہ ہوتا تو چاہیے تھا کہ ہر وصیت کرنے والے کا مال اس کے اختیار سے نکل کر رکا رہتا حالانکہ ایسا نہیں ہے کبھی آدمی اپنی صحت کو بدل سکتا ہے سوائے تدبیر کے۔ کہا مالک نے ہمارے نزدیک ہر وصیت کو بدل سکتا ہے سائے تدبیر کے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1455]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1455" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya, nahi layak hai aadmi ko jiske paas cheez ya mamla aisa ho jisme wasiyat karna zaroori ho aur wo do raatein guzare baghair wasiyat likhe huye. Malik ne kaha agar mosi apni wasiyat ke badalne par qadir na hota to chahiye tha ke har wasiyat karne wale ka maal uske ikhtiyar se nikal kar ruka rehta, halanki aisa nahi hai, kabhi aadmi apni sehat ko badal sakta hai siwaye tadbeer ke. Malik ne kaha hamare nazdeek har wasiyat ko badal sakta hai siwaye tadbeer ke.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1455]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1455" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>
